		client                    - draw client world(s)
		server                    - draw server world(s)
		other                     - draw other world(s) - useful in tools
		wireframe                 - draw wireframe
		solid                     - draw solid (not wireframe, with tool textures)
		plain                     - draw solid (not wireframe, plain debug colors)
		transparent 128           - draw transparent (0-255)
		nodraw | off              - draw nothing
		{collisionGroup, interactAs, interactWith, interactExclude, targetDetailLayer, includedDetailLayers} #FFFFFFFF
		layer #401F or layer401F  - debug layer mask
		debugName substring       - filter by substring in debug name
		stackFilter substring     - filter traces by callstack strings
		not                       - invert filter
		surface slipperymetal     - filter by surface names or (#41BC2B3D) hash
		trigger|nav|clip          - draw corresponding tool materials
		cfFF                      - collision function mask
